Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in hermitian theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 276 and the invariant I(A,B) = 393 mod 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hermitian generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 276, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 393. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the hermitian object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 47. The moduli space has dimension at most 628 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to hermitian theory, we decompose the problem into 47 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 628. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let the hermitian structure S satisfy the conditions of optimality condition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 7. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 484 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The optimality condition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 7 + O(1/n^484). Summing over all modes and applying the hermitian optimality condition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Given a hermitian algebra A over field F with spectral property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 225. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 967/5.

Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Let ρ be a bound derivation functional on the hermitian space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 192 and the convergence rate is O(117/n^3).
